--- Commit Message ---
LU-19917 mgc: dynamic MGS nids discovery

. enable fault tolerant MGS, client can connect to any server as MGS
. MGS nid list can be specified in client mount command, and they
  will be shuffled to balance load, the benefit of MGS nid list is
  that the system startup time is reduced for large clusters, and the
  MGS load is balanced among all servers
. imperative recovery log process adds all server nids to client
  MGC import dynamically, so that user can start using fault tolerant
  MGS without any configuration
. server MGC name should be different from client MGC, because server
  MGC only connects to primary MGS, rename the server device to
  "mgc<NID>", update conf-sanity and recovery-small for this change
. shorten MGC ping interval to
  max(PING_INTERVAL_SHORT, obd_timeout/20) so that client switches
  MGS in time to avoid missing IR update
. add conf-sanity.sh test_163

Test-Parameters: mdtcount=4 testlist=conf-sanity serverversion=2.17
Test-Parameters: mdtcount=4 testlist=conf-sanity clientversion=2.17
Test-Parameters: mdtcount=4 testlist=recovery-small env=ONLY=141,HONOR_EXCEPT=y serverversion=2.17
Test-Parameters: mdtcount=4 testlist=recovery-small env=ONLY=141,HONOR_EXCEPT=y clientversion=2.17
Signed-off-by: Lai Siyao <lai.siyao@whamcloud.com>

Some concerns, see inline comments. The main ones are in mgc_request.c: the new client_import_add_nids_to_conn() block in mgc_apply_recover_logs() re-takes the MGC cl_sem that do_requeue() already holds for read, and the new local lock cancel in do_requeue() re-arms cld_lostlock through the synchronous cancel callback so the requeue never settles. It would also be easier to review if the MGC lock-handling fixes (do_config_log_add() label, do_requeue(), mgc_requeue_add()) and the mgs_nids.c assertion change were split out from the FTM feature itself.
